### 星际行动 ###
gts_leader_colossus_effect = {
	potential = {
		always = yes
	}
	allow = {
		#星际行动限制
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "gts_leader_interact_no_intro"
			has_global_flag = gts_leader_interact_intro
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "gts_gateway_final_needed"
			from = {
				has_megastructure = gts_gateway_final
				has_country_flag = gts_gateway_situation_done
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "gts_leader_interact_cooldown"
			from = {
				NOR = {
					has_country_flag = gts_leader_interact_cooldown
					has_country_flag = gts_leader_interact_active
					has_country_flag = gts_leader_interact_storm_coming
				}
			}
		}
		gts_ui_leader_panel_lock = yes
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = gts_leader_colossus.tooltip
		country_event = {
			id = gts_ui.10
		}
	}
}
### 换衣服 ###
gts_leader_cloth_effect = {
	potential = {
		always = yes
	}
	allow = {
		gts_ui_leader_panel_lock = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "gts_leader_no_alter_clothes"
			event_target:current_gts_leader = {
				has_leader_flag = gts_leader_has_alter_cloth
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = gts_leader_cloth.tooltip
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
				id = gts_ui.11
			}
		}
	}
}
### 接入通讯 ###
gts_leader_communication_effect = {
	potential = {
		always = yes
	}
	allow = {
		gts_ui_leader_panel_lock = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			fail_text = "gts_leader_no_communication"
			event_target:current_gts_leader = {
				has_leader_flag = gts_leader_has_communication
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = gts_leader_communication.tooltip
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
				id = gts_ui.12
			}
		}
	}
}

# gts_ui_current_slot_class
# 0 = 未分配
# 1 = 宠物
# 2 = 道具
gts_leader_item_1_effect = {
	potential = {
		always = yes
	}
	allow = {
		gts_ui_leader_panel_lock = yes
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = gts_leader_item_1.tooltip
		gts_ui_item_in_slot_click = { SLOT = 1 CLASS = 2 }
	}
}

gts_leader_body_1_effect = {
	potential = {
		or = {
			event_target:current_gts_leader = {
				has_leader_flag = gts_xiaomiao
			}
			check_variable = {
				which = gts_ui_intel_avatar
				value = @gts_xiaomiao
			}
		}
	}
	allow = {
		always = yes
	}
	effect = {}
}

gts_leader_item_i_assembly_slot_1_effect = {
	potential = {
		gts_ui_leader_display_item = { SLOT = 1 ITEM = @gts_leader_item_i_assembly }
	}
	allow = {
		gts_ui_leader_panel_lock = yes
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = gts_leader_item_i_assembly.tooltip
		gts_ui_item_in_slot_click = { SLOT = 1 CLASS = 2 }
	}
}

gts_leader_pet_botton_open_effect = {
	potential = {

	}
	allow = {
		from = {
			not = {
				has_country_flag = gts_leader_pet_open
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		from = {
			set_country_flag = gts_leader_pet_open
		}
	}
}
gts_leader_pet_botton_close_effect = {
	potential = {
		from = {
			has_country_flag = gts_leader_pet_open
		}
	}
	allow = {

	}
	effect = {
		from = {
			remove_country_flag = gts_leader_pet_open
		}
	}
}

gts_leader_decorations_back_effect = {
	# 向后翻页
	potential = {
		always = yes
	}
	allow = {
		hidden_trigger = {
			from = {
				check_variable = {
					which = gts_ui_leader_decorations_page
					value > 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = gts_ui_back.tooltip
		from = {
			hidden_effect = {
				subtract_variable = {
					which = gts_ui_leader_decorations_page
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
gts_leader_decorations_next_effect = {
	# 向前翻页
	potential = {
		always = yes
	}
	allow = {
		hidden_trigger = {
			from = {
				check_variable = {
					which = gts_ui_leader_decorations_page
					value < 10
				}
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = gts_ui_next.tooltip
		from = {
			hidden_effect = {
				change_variable = {
					which = gts_ui_leader_decorations_page
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#####################################################     装配    #####################################################################################

gts_leader_decorations_up_1_effect = {
	potential = {
		from = {
			check_variable = {
				which = gts_ui_leader_decorations_page
				value = 1
			}
		}
		event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
			not = {
				has_leader_flag = bfbf_decorations_test_1
			}
		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip_fail = {
			text = gts_leader_item_i_bfbf_assembly_effect_allow
			from = {
				check_variable = {
					which = gts_leader_decorations_1
					value > 0
				}
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
				set_leader_flag = bfbf_decorations_test_1
			}
			from = {
				add_variable = {
					which = gts_leader_decorations_1
					value = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
gts_leader_decorations_up_2_effect = {
	potential = {
		from = {
			check_variable = {
				which = gts_ui_leader_decorations_page
				value = 1
			}
		}
		event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
			not = {
				has_leader_flag = bfbf_decorations_test_2
			}
		}
	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip_fail = {
			text = gts_leader_item_i_bfbf_assembly_effect_allow
			from = {
				check_variable = {
					which = gts_leader_decorations_2
					value > 0
				}
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {

gts_leader_decorations_build_1_effect = {
	potential = {
		from = {
			check_variable = {
				which = gts_ui_leader_decorations_page
				value = 1
			}
		}

	}
	allow = {
		custom_tooltip_fail = {
			text = gts_leader_item_i_bfbf_build_allow1
			from = {
				has_technology = tech_mega_engineering
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip_fail = {
			text = gts_leader_item_i_bfbf_build_allow2
			from = {
				#has_megastructure = 占位符
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip_fail = {
			text = gts_leader_item_i_bfbf_build_allow3
			from = {
				resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = unity value >= 6000 }
				resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = alloys value >= 20000 }
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		hidden_effect = {
			from = {
				country_event = {
					id = gts_bfbf.12001
					days = 0#记得改
				}
			}
		}
		add_resource = {
			unity = -6000
		}
		add_resource = {
			alloys = -20000
		}
	}
}

			text = gts_leader_item_i_bfbf_build_allow3
			from = {
				resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = unity value >= 6000 }
				resource_stockpile_compare = { resource = alloys value >= 20000 }
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		hidden_effect = {
			from = {
				country_event = {
					id = gts_bfbf.12016
					days = 0#记得改
				}
			}
		}
		add_resource = {
			unity = -6000
		}
		add_resource = {
			alloys = -20000
		}
	}
}

gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_1_effect = {
	potential = {
		event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
			leader_class = scientist
			not = {
				has_leader_flag = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_1
			}
		}
	}
	allow = {
		event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
			not = {
				has_leader_flag = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill >= 1
					has_base_skill < 5
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_1_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_main
						value <= 0
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill >= 5
					has_base_skill < 10
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_1_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_main
						value <= 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill >= 10
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_1_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_main
						value <= 2
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_1_effect
		event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
			set_leader_flag = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_1
			add_variable = {
				which = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_main
				value = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

						which = trait_leader_gts_skill
						value < 3
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {

				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill = 4
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_01_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = trait_leader_gts_skill
						value < 4
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill = 5
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_01_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = trait_leader_gts_skill
						value < 5
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill = 6
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_01_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = trait_leader_gts_skill
						value < 6
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill = 7
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_01_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = trait_leader_gts_skill
						value < 7
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill = 8
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_01_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = trait_leader_gts_skill
						value < 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill = 9
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_01_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = trait_leader_gts_skill
						value < 9
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill >= 10
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_01_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = trait_leader_gts_skill
						value < 10
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = trait_leader_gts_skill_scientist_15_effect
		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
				set_leader_flag = gts_skill_scientist_level_15
				add_variable = {
					which = trait_leader_gts_skill
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_official_1_effect = {
	potential = {
		event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
			leader_class = official
			not = {
				has_leader_flag = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_official_1
			}
		}
	}
	allow = {
		event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
			not = {
				has_leader_flag = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_official_1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill >= 1
					has_base_skill < 5
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_official_1_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_main
						value <= 0
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill >= 5
					has_base_skill < 10
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_official_1_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_main
						value <= 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill >= 10
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_official_1_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_main
						value <= 2
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = trait_leader_gts_skill_official_1_effect
		event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
			set_leader_flag = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_official_1
			add_variable = {
				which = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_main
				value = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_commander_1_effect = {
	potential = {
		event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
			leader_class = commander
			not = {
				has_leader_flag = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_commander_1
			}
		}
	}
	allow = {
		event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
			not = {
				has_leader_flag = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_commander_1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill >= 1
					has_base_skill < 5
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_commander_1_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_main
						value <= 0
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill >= 5
					has_base_skill < 10
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_commander_1_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_main
						value <= 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					has_base_skill >= 10
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = trait_leader_gts_skill_commander_1_text
				event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
					check_variable = {
						which = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_main
						value <= 2
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	effect = {
		custom_tooltip = trait_leader_gts_skill_commander_1_effect
		event_target:gts_bfbf_love_target = {
			set_leader_flag = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_commander_1
			add_variable = {
				which = gts_trait_leader_gts_skill_main
				value = 1
			}
		}
	}
}
